Closed form.

The right chain reads O[i] = I[i-4] AND I[i-2] for bits 4..7, with O[0..3] = 0. That is exactly a logical right shift by 4 ANDed with a logical right shift by 2:

    Output = (Input >> 4) AND (Input >> 2)

since positions 0..3 of both shifts are zero (bits filled in from the left), their AND is zero there, and for i in 4..7 the shifted values contribute x[i-4] and x[i-2].
